#SSTOP_LOOP
#MSTOP

#黒
#飛んでいく
#IF $997!0
#GOTO EXTRA
#ENDIF
#RETURN

#SCREEN_DAY
#SSTOP
#SSTOP_LOOP
#MSTOP
#VSTOP
#EFFECT_SWITCH 1
#黒
#PUT_TEXTPLANE 1,1
#BG+CHR_DARK
#SCRCHNG=02,12
#BG+CHR
#PUTA=1,9,2,0,0,800,600,0,0,0,0,255,
#EFFECT_SWITCH 0
#IF $997!0
/#GOTO TITLE_START
#GOTO EXTRA
#ENDIF
#RETURN
